Common patterns for invoice data\n    vendor_pattern = r'(?:Vendor|Supplier|From|Billed By)[:\\s]*([A-Za-z0-9\\s\\.\\-&,]+)'\n    amount_pattern = r'(?:Amount|Total|Grand Total)[:\\s]*\\$?([0-9,]+\\.[0-9]{2})'\n    iban_pattern = r'\\b([A-Z]{2}[0-9]{2}[A-Z0-9]{11,30})\\b'\n    po_pattern = r'(?:PO|Purchase Order)[:\\s]*#?([A-Z0-9-]+)'\n\n    # Extract fields\n    vendor_match = re.search(vendor_pattern, invoice_text, re.IGNORECASE)\n    amount_match = re.search(amount_pattern, invoice_text, re.IGNORECASE)\n    iban_match = re.search(iban_pattern, invoice_text)\n    po_match = re.search(po_pattern, invoice_text, re.IGNORECASE)\n\n    vendor_name = vendor_match.group(1).strip() if vendor_match else \"\"\n    # Clean up vendor name (remove common suffixes that might be in separate lines)\n    vendor_name = re.sub(r'\\s+', ' ', vendor_name)\n\n    amount_str = amount_match.group(1).replace(',', '') if amount_match else \"0\"\n    try:\n        amount = float(amount_str)\n    except:\n        amount = 0.0\n\n    iban = iban_match.group(1) if iban_match else \"\"\n    po_number = po_match.group(1) if po_match else \"\"\n\n    return {\n        'invoice_page_number': page_num,\n        'vendor_name': vendor_name,\n        'invoice_amount': amount,\n        'iban': iban,\n        'po_number': po_number if po_number else None\n    }\n\ndef find_best_vendor_match(invoice_vendor, vendor_list, threshold=0.8):\n    \"\"\"Find best matching vendor from vendor list using fuzzy matching\"\"\"\n    best_match = None\n    best_score = 0\n\n    for _, vendor_row in vendor_list.iterrows():\n        vendor_name = str(vendor_row['Name'])\n        score = similarity(invoice_vendor, vendor_name)\n        if score > best_score:\n            best_score = score\n            best_match = vendor_row\n\n    if best_score >= threshold:\n        return best_match, best_score\n    return None, 0\n\ndef check_fraud(invoice, vendors_df, po_df):\n    \"\"\"Check if invoice is fraudulent based on criteria\"\"\"\n    vendor_name = invoice['vendor_name']\n    invoice_iban = invoice['iban']\n    po_number = invoice['po_number']\n    invoice_amount = invoice['invoice_amount']\n\n    # Criterion 1: Unknown Vendor (with fuzzy matching)\n    best_match, score = find_best_vendor_match(vendor_name, vendors_df)\n    if best_match is None:\n        return {\n            'invoice_page_number': invoice['invoice_page_number'],\n            'vendor_name': vendor_name,\n            'invoice_amount': invoice_amount,\n            'iban': invoice_iban,\n            'po_number': po_number,\n            'reason': 'Unknown Vendor'\n        }\n\n    vendor_id = best_match['Vendor ID']\n    vendor_iban = best_match['IBAN']\n\n    # Criterion 2: IBAN Mismatch\n    if invoice_iban and vendor_iban and invoice_iban != vendor_iban:\n        return {\n            'invoice_page_number': invoice['invoice_page_number'],\n            'vendor_name': vendor_name,\n            'invoice_amount': invoice_amount,\n            'iban': invoice_iban,\n            'po_number': po_number,\n            'reason': 'IBAN Mismatch'\n        }\n\n    # Criterion 3: Invalid PO\n    if po_number:\n        po_match = po_df[po_df['PO Number'] == po_number]\n        if po_match.empty:\n            return {\n                'invoice_page_number': invoice['invoice_page_number'],\n                'vendor_name': vendor_name,\n                'invoice_amount': invoice_amount,\n                'iban': invoice_iban,\n                'po_number': po_number,\n                'reason': 'Invalid PO'\n            }\n\n        po_row = po_match.iloc[0]\n        po_vendor_id = po_row['Vendor ID']\n        po_amount = po_row['Amount']\n\n        # Criterion 4: Amount Mismatch\n        if abs(invoice_amount - po_amount) > 0.01:\n            return {\n                'invoice_page_number': invoice['invoice_page_number'],\n                'vendor_name': vendor_name,\n                'invoice_amount': invoice_amount,\n                'iban': invoice_iban,\n                'po_number': po_number,\n                'reason': 'Amount Mismatch'\n            }\n\n        # Criterion 5: Vendor Mismatch\n        if vendor_id != po_vendor_id:\n            return {\n                'invoice_page_number': invoice['invoice_page_number'],\n                'vendor_name': vendor_name,\n                'invoice_amount': invoice_amount,\n                'iban': invoice_iban,\n                'po_number': po_number,\n                'reason': 'Vendor Mismatch'\n            }\n\n    # No fraud detected\n    return None\n\ndef main():\n    # File paths\n    pdf_path = '/root/invoices.pdf'\n    vendors_path = '/root/vendors.xlsx'\n    po_path = '/root/purchase_orders.csv'\n    output_path = '/root/fraud_report.json'\n\n    # Load data\n    print(\"Loading vendors data...\")\n    vendors_df = pd.read_excel(vendors_path)\n    print(f\"Loaded {len(vendors_df)} vendors\")\n\n    print(\"Loading purchase orders data...\")\n    po_df = pd.read_csv(po_path)\n    print(f\"Loaded {len(po_df)} purchase orders\")\n\n    # Extract invoices from PDF\n    print(\"Extracting invoices from PDF...\")\n    invoice_pages = extract_text_from_pdf(pdf_path)\n    print(f\"Found {len(invoice_pages)} invoice pages\")\n\n    # Parse invoices\n    invoices = []\n    for page_data in invoice_pages:\n        invoice = parse_invoice_data(page_data['text'], page_data['page_number'])\n        invoices.append(invoice)\n\n    # Check for fraud\n    print(\"Analyzing invoices for fraud...\")\n    fraud_cases = []\n    for invoice in invoices:\n        fraud_result = check_fraud(invoice, vendors_df, po_df)\n        if fraud_result:\n            fraud_cases.append(fraud_result)\n\n    # Save report\n    print(f\"Found {len(fraud_cases)} fraudulent invoices\")\n    with open(output_path, 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(fraud_cases, f, indent=2)\n\n    print(f\"Fraud report saved to {output_path}\")\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    main()\n"}}],"model":"MiniMax-M2","stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":191,"output_tokens":0,"cache_creation_input_tokens":694,"cache_read_input_tokens":16978},"service_tier":"standard","context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"afbf88b2-0225-43fb-bc51-da5d83af9b36","uuid":"f28b0f31-6888-4c81-9cd5-3e32ebf5889b"}

Common patterns for invoice data\n    vendor_pattern = r'(?:Vendor|Supplier|From|Billed By)[:\\s]*([A-Za-z0-9\\s\\.\\-&,]+)'\n    amount_pattern = r'(?:Amount|Total|Grand Total)[:\\s]*\\$?([0-9,]+\\.[0-9]{2})'\n    iban_pattern = r'\\b([A-Z]{2}[0-9]{2}[A-Z0-9]{11,30})\\b'\n    po_pattern = r'(?:PO|Purchase Order)[:\\s]*#?([A-Z0-9-]+)'\n\n    # Extract fields\n    vendor_match = re.search(vendor_pattern, invoice_text, re.IGNORECASE)\n    amount_match = re.search(amount_pattern, invoice_text, re.IGNORECASE)\n    iban_match = re.search(iban_pattern, invoice_text)\n    po_match = re.search(po_pattern, invoice_text, re.IGNORECASE)\n\n    vendor_name = vendor_match.group(1).strip() if vendor_match else \"\"\n    # Criterion 1: Unknown Vendor (with fuzzy matching)\n    best_match, score = find_best_vendor_match(vendor_name, vendors_df)\n    if best_match is None:\n        return {\n            'invoice_page_number': invoice['invoice_page_number'],\n            'vendor_name': vendor_name,\n            'invoice_amount': invoice_amount,\n            'iban': invoice_iban,\n            'po_number': po_number,\n            'reason': 'Unknown Vendor'\n        }\n\n    vendor_id = best_match['id']\n    vendor_iban = best_match['iban']\n\n    # Criterion 2: IBAN Mismatch\n    if invoice_iban and vendor_iban and invoice_iban != vendor_iban:\n        return {\n            'invoice_page_number': invoice['invoice_page_number'],\n            'vendor_name': vendor_name,\n            'invoice_amount': invoice_amount,\n            'iban': invoice_iban,\n            'po_number': po_number,\n            'reason': 'IBAN Mismatch'\n        }\n\n    # Criterion 3: Invalid PO\n    if po_number:\n        po_match = po_df[po_df['po_number'] == po_number]\n        if po_match.empty:\n            return {\n                'invoice_page_number': invoice['invoice_page_number'],\n                'vendor_name': vendor_name,\n                'invoice_amount': invoice_amount,\n                'iban': invoice_iban,\n                'po_number': po_number,\n                'reason': 'Invalid PO'\n            }\n\n        po_row = po_match.iloc[0]\n        po_vendor_id = po_row['vendor_id']\n        po_amount = po_row['amount']\n\n        # Criterion 4: Amount Mismatch\n        if abs(invoice_amount - po_amount) > 0.01:\n            return {\n                'invoice_page_number': invoice['invoice_page_number'],\n                'vendor_name': vendor_name,\n                'invoice_amount': invoice_amount,\n                'iban': invoice_iban,\n                'po_number': po_number,\n                'reason': 'Amount Mismatch'\n            }\n\n        # Criterion 5: Vendor Mismatch\n        if vendor_id != po_vendor_id:\n            return {\n                'invoice_page_number': invoice['invoice_page_number'],\n                'vendor_name': vendor_name,\n                'invoice_amount': invoice_amount,\n                'iban': invoice_iban,\n                'po_number': po_number,\n                'reason': 'Vendor Mismatch'\n            }\n\n    # Check for fraud\n    print(\"Analyzing invoices for fraud...\")\n    fraud_cases = []\n    for invoice in invoices:\n        fraud_result = check_fraud(invoice, vendors_df, po_df)\n        if fraud_result:\n            fraud_cases.append(fraud_result)\n\n    # Save report\n    print(f\"Found {len(fraud_cases)} fraudulent invoices\")\n    with open(output_path, 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(fraud_cases, f, indent=2)\n\n    print(f\"Fraud report saved to {output_path}\")\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    main()\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":30,"oldLines":21,"newStart":30,"newLines":19,"lines":[" "," def parse_invoice_data(invoice_text, page_num):","     \"\"\"Parse invoice data from extracted text\"\"\"","-    #
